<div class="mw-body-content" id="mw-content-text"><div class="mw-subjectpageheader">
</div><div class="mw-content-ltr mw-parser-output" data-mw-html-version="2.8.0" data-mw-parsoid-version="0.24.0.0-alpha14" dir="ltr" lang="en"><section data-mw-section-id="0" id="mwAQ"><p class="mw-empty-elt" id="mwAg"><span about="#mwt1" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Short description","href":"./Template:Short_description"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"none"}},"i":0}}]}' id="mwAw" typeof="mw:Nowiki mw:Transclusion"></span><link about="#mwt1" href="./Category:Articles_with_short_description" rel="mw:PageProp/Category"/><link about="#mwt1" href="./Category:Short_description_is_different_from_Wikidata" id="mwBA" rel="mw:PageProp/Category"/></p>
<span about="#mwt2" class="mw-empty-elt" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"redirect","href":"./Template:Redirect"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Presidents of the United States"},"2":{"wt":""},"3":{"wt":"President of the United States (disambiguation)"}},"i":0}}]}' id="mwBQ" typeof="mw:Transclusion"><style about="#mwt3" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Hatnote/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}' data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1353705441" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles">.mw-parser-output .hatnote{font-style:italic}.mw-parser-output div.hatnote{padding-left:1.6em;margin-bottom:0.5em}.mw-parser-output .hatnote i{font-style:normal}.mw-parser-output .hatnote+span.mw-empty-elt+.hatnote,.mw-parser-output .hatnote+link+.hatnote{margin-top:-0.5em}@media print{body.ns-0 .mw-parser-output .hatnote{display:none!important}}</style></span><div about="#mwt2" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" id="mwBg" role="note">"Presidents of the United States" redirects here. For other uses, see <a class="mw-disambig" href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/President_of_the_United_States_(disambiguation)" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="President of the United States (disambiguation)">President of the United States (disambiguation)</a>.</div>
<p class="mw-empty-elt" id="mwBw"><span about="#mwt4" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"featured list","href":"./Template:Featured_list"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}' id="mwCA" typeof="mw:Nowiki mw:Transclusion"></span><meta about="#mwt4" data-mw='{"name":"indicator","attrs":{"name":"featured-star"},"body":{"extsrc":"[[File:Cscr-featured.svg|20x20px |link=Wikipedia:Featured lists   |This is a featured list. Click here for more information.]]\n"}}' id="mwCQ" typeof="mw:Extension/indicator"/><link about="#mwt4" href="./Category:Featured_lists" id="mwCg" rel="mw:PageProp/Category"/>
<span about="#mwt6" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"pp-extended","href":"./Template:Pp-extended"},"params":{"small":{"wt":"yes"}},"i":0}}]}' id="mwCw" typeof="mw:Nowiki mw:Transclusion"></span><meta about="#mwt6" data-mw='{"name":"indicator","attrs":{"name":"pp-default"},"body":{"extsrc":"[[File:Extended-protection-shackle.svg|20px|link=Wikipedia:Protection policy#extended|alt=Extended-protected article|This article is extended-confirmed-protected.]]"}}' id="mwDA" typeof="mw:Extension/indicator"/><link about="#mwt6" href="./Category:Wikipedia_extended-confirmed-protected_pages#List%20of%20presidents%20of%20the%20United%20States" id="mwDQ" rel="mw:PageProp/Category"/>
<span about="#mwt8" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Use American English","href":"./Template:Use_American_English"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"June 2021"}},"i":0}}]}' id="mwDg" typeof="mw:Nowiki mw:Transclusion"></span><link about="#mwt8" href="./Category:Use_American_English_from_June_2021" rel="mw:PageProp/Category"/><link about="#mwt8" href="./Category:All_Wikipedia_articles_written_in_American_English" id="mwDw" rel="mw:PageProp/Category"/>
<span about="#mwt9" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Use mdy dates","href":"./Template:Use_mdy_dates"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"April 2017"}},"i":0}}]}' id="mwEA" typeof="mw:Nowiki mw:Transclusion"></span><link about="#mwt9" href="./Category:Use_mdy_dates_from_April_2017" id="mwEQ" rel="mw:PageProp/Category"/></p>
<figure id="mwEg" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a class="mw-file-description" href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:White_House_north_and_south_sides.jpg" id="mwEw"><img class="mw-file-element" data-file-height="5056" data-file-type="bitmap" data-file-width="4418" decoding="async" height="339" id="mwFA" resource="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:White_House_north_and_south_sides.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/1d/White_House_north_and_south_sides.jpg/330px-White_House_north_and_south_sides.jpg" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/1d/White_House_north_and_south_sides.jpg/960px-White_House_north_and_south_sides.jpg 2x" width="296"/></a><figcaption id="mwFQ">The <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/White_House" id="mwFg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="White House">White House</a>'s north and south sides, official residence of the president of the United States</figcaption></figure>
<p id="mwFw">The <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/President_of_the_United_States" id="mwGA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="President of the United States">president of the United States</a> is the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Head_of_state" id="mwGQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Head of state">head of state</a> and <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Head_of_government" id="mwGg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Head of government">head of government</a> of the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States" id="mwGw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="United States">United States</a>,<sup about="#mwt10" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTERossiter196286"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTERossiter196286-1"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Rossiter"},"2":{"wt":"1962"},"p":{"wt":"86"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTERossiter196286_1-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTERossiter196286-1" id="mwHA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwHQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwHg">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwHw">]</span></span></a></sup> <a class="mw-redirect" href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indirectly_elected" id="mwIA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Indirectly elected">indirectly elected</a> to a four-year <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Term_of_office" id="mwIQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Term of office">term</a> via the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_Electoral_College" id="mwIg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="United States Electoral College">Electoral College</a>.<sup about="#mwt13" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEShugart2004633–636"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEShugart2004633–636-2"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Shugart"},"2":{"wt":"2004"},"pp":{"wt":"633–636"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEShugart2004633–636_2-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEShugart2004633–636-2" id="mwIw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwJA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJQ">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJg">]</span></span></a></sup> Under the <a class="mw-redirect" href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/U.S._Constitution" id="mwJw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="U.S. Constitution">U.S. Constitution</a>, the officeholder leads the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Executive_(government)" id="mwKA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Executive (government)">executive branch</a> of the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Federal_government_of_the_United_States" id="mwKQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Federal government of the United States">federal government</a> and is the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Commander-in-chief" id="mwKg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Commander-in-chief">commander-in-chief</a> of the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_Armed_Forces" id="mwKw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="United States Armed Forces">United States Armed Forces</a>.<sup about="#mwt16" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEEpstein2005318"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEEpstein2005318-3"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Epstein"},"2":{"wt":"2005"},"p":{"wt":"318"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEEpstein2005318_3-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEEpstein2005318-3" id="mwLA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwLQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwLg">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwLw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<p id="mwMA">The first president, <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Washington" id="mwMQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="George Washington">George Washington</a>, won a unanimous vote of the Electoral College.<sup about="#mwt19" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEMatuz2001xxii"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEMatuz2001xxii-4"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Matuz"},"2":{"wt":"2001"},"p":{"wt":"xxii"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEMatuz2001xxii_4-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEMatuz2001xxii-4" id="mwMg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwMw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNA">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNQ">]</span></span></a></sup> The incumbent president is <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Donald_Trump" id="mwNg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Donald Trump">Donald Trump</a>, who <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Second_inauguration_of_Donald_Trump" id="mwNw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Second inauguration of Donald Trump">assumed office on January 20, 2025</a>.<sup about="#mwt22" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEHajela2024"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEHajela2024-5"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Hajela"},"2":{"wt":"2024"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEHajela2024_5-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEHajela2024-5" id="mwOA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwOQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwOg">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwOw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt25" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEFreile2024"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEFreile2024-6"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Freile"},"2":{"wt":"2024"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEFreile2024_6-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEFreile2024-6" id="mwPA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwPQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwPg">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwPw">]</span></span></a></sup> Since the office was established in 1789, 45 men have served in 47 presidencies. The discrepancy is due to the nonconsecutive terms of <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grover_Cleveland" id="mwQA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Grover Cleveland">Grover Cleveland</a> (counted as the 22nd and 24th president) and Trump (counted as the 45th and 47th president).<sup about="#mwt28" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEAP2024"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEAP2024-7"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"AP"},"2":{"wt":"2024"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEAP2024_7-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEAP2024-7" id="mwQQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwQg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwQw">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt31" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTESchallerWilliams2003192"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTESchallerWilliams2003192-8"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Schaller"},"2":{"wt":"Williams"},"3":{"wt":"2003"},"p":{"wt":"192"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTESchallerWilliams2003192_8-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTESchallerWilliams2003192-8" id="mwRQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwRg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRw">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<p id="mwSQ">The presidency of <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Henry_Harrison" id="mwSg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="William Henry Harrison">William Henry Harrison</a>, who died 31 days after taking office in 1841, was the shortest in American history.<sup about="#mwt34" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEMcHughMackowiak2014990–995"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcHughMackowiak2014990–995-9"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"McHugh"},"2":{"wt":"Mackowiak"},"3":{"wt":"2014"},"pp":{"wt":"990–995"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEMcHughMackowiak2014990–995_9-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcHughMackowiak2014990–995-9" id="mwSw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwTA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTQ">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTg">]</span></span></a></sup> <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Franklin_D._Roosevelt" id="mwTw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Franklin D. Roosevelt">Franklin D. Roosevelt</a> served the longest, over twelve years, before dying early in his fourth term in 1945. He is the only U.S. president to have served more than two terms.<sup about="#mwt37" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTESkau1974246–275"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTESkau1974246–275-10"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Skau"},"2":{"wt":"1974"},"pp":{"wt":"246–275"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTESkau1974246–275_10-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTESkau1974246–275-10" id="mwUA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwUQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUg">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUw">]</span></span></a></sup> Since the ratification of the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Twenty-second_Amendment_to_the_United_States_Constitution" id="mwVA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Twenty-second Amendment to the United States Constitution">Twenty-second Amendment to the United States Constitution</a> in 1951, no person may be elected president more than twice, and no one who has served more than two years of a term to which someone else was elected may be elected more than once.<sup about="#mwt40" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEPeabodyGant1999565"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEPeabodyGant1999565-11"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Peabody"},"2":{"wt":"Gant"},"3":{"wt":"1999"},"p":{"wt":"565"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEPeabodyGant1999565_11-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEPeabodyGant1999565-11" id="mwVQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwVg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVw">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<p id="mwWQ">Four presidents died in office of natural causes (William Henry Harrison, <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zachary_Taylor" id="mwWg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Zachary Taylor">Zachary Taylor</a>, <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Warren_G._Harding" id="mwWw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Warren G. Harding">Warren G. Harding</a>, and Franklin D. Roosevelt), four were <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_United_States_presidential_assassination_attempts_and_plots" id="mwXA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="List of United States presidential assassination attempts and plots">assassinated</a> (<a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abraham_Lincoln" id="mwXQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Abraham Lincoln">Abraham Lincoln</a>, <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_A._Garfield" id="mwXg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="James A. Garfield">James A. Garfield</a>, <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_McKinley" id="mwXw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="William McKinley">William McKinley</a>, and <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_F._Kennedy" id="mwYA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="John F. Kennedy">John F. Kennedy</a>), and one resigned (<a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Richard_Nixon" id="mwYQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Richard Nixon">Richard Nixon</a>, facing <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Impeachment_process_against_Richard_Nixon" id="mwYg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Impeachment process against Richard Nixon">impeachment</a> and removal from office).<sup about="#mwt43" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEAbbott2005627–644"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005627–644-12"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Abbott"},"2":{"wt":"2005"},"pp":{"wt":"627–644"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005627–644_12-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005627–644-12" id="mwYw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwZA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwZQ">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwZg">]</span></span></a></sup> <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Tyler" id="mwZw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="John Tyler">John Tyler</a> was the first <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vice_President_of_the_United_States" id="mwaA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Vice President of the United States">vice president</a> to assume the presidency during a presidential term, setting the precedent that a vice president who does so becomes the fully functioning president with a new, distinct administration.<sup about="#mwt46" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEDinnerstein1962447–451"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEDinnerstein1962447–451-13"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Dinnerstein"},"2":{"wt":"1962"},"pp":{"wt":"447–451"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEDinnerstein1962447–451_13-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEDinnerstein1962447–451-13" id="mwaQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwag"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwaw">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwbA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<p id="mwbQ">Throughout most of its history, <a class="mw-redirect" href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/American_politics" id="mwbg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="American politics">American politics</a> has been dominated by <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Political_parties_in_the_United_States" id="mwbw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Political parties in the United States">political parties</a>. The Constitution is silent on the issue of political parties, and at the time it came into force in 1789, no organized parties existed. Soon after the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1st_United_States_Congress" id="mwcA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="1st United States Congress">1st Congress</a> convened, <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Political_faction" id="mwcQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Political faction">political factions</a> began rallying around dominant <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Presidency_of_George_Washington" id="mwcg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Presidency of George Washington">Washington administration</a> officials, such as <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alexander_Hamilton" id="mwcw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Alexander Hamilton">Alexander Hamilton</a> and <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thomas_Jefferson" id="mwdA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Thomas Jefferson">Thomas Jefferson</a>.<sup about="#mwt49" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;group&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;name&quot;:&quot;FOOTNOTE''Guide to U.S. Elections''2010197Nardulli1992179&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010197Nardulli1992179-14&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Sfnmp&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Sfnmp&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;''Guide to U.S. Elections''&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;2010&quot;},&quot;1p&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;197&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Nardulli&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;1992&quot;},&quot;2p&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;179&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010197Nardulli1992179_14-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010197Nardulli1992179-14" id="mwdQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwdg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwdw">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mweA">]</span></span></a></sup> Concerned about the capacity of political parties to destroy the fragile unity holding the nation together, Washington remained <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Independent_politician" id="mweQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Independent politician">unaffiliated</a> with any political faction or party throughout his eight-year presidency. He remains the only U.S. president who never affiliated with a political party.<sup about="#mwt52" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTELOC (2)Jamison2014"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTELOC_(2)Jamison2014-15"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnmp","href":"./Template:Sfnmp"},"params":{"1a1":{"wt":"LOC (2)"},"2a1":{"wt":"Jamison"},"2y":{"wt":"2014"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTELOC_(2)Jamison2014_15-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTELOC_(2)Jamison2014-15" id="mweg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwew"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwfA">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwfQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<meta data-mw='{"autoGenerated":true}' id="mwfg" property="mw:PageProp/toc"/></section><section aria-labelledby="Presidents" data-mw-section-id="1" id="mwfw"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Presidents">Presidents</h2></div>

For example, George Washington served two consecutive terms and is counted as the first president, instead of the first and second, but Grover Cleveland and Donald Trump are counted twice because their two terms were not consecutive. A vice president who temporarily becomes [[Acting President of the United States|acting president]] under the [[Twenty-fifth Amendment to the United States Constitution|Twenty-fifth Amendment]] to the Constitution is not counted, because the president remains in office during such a period.&lt;!-- Citation not required; general note about the table. --&gt;"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-16" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_note-16" id="mwig"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwiw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwjA">[</span>a<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwjQ">]</span></span></a></sup></th>
<th class="unsortable" id="mwjg" scope="col">Portrait</th>
<th id="mwjw" scope="col">Name<br id="mwkA"/><span about="#mwt64" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"small","href":"./Template:Small"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"(birth–death)"}},"i":0}}]}' id="mwkQ" style="font-size: 85%;" typeof="mw:Transclusion">(birth–death)</span></th>
<th class="unsortable" id="mwkg" scope="col">Term<sup about="#mwt65" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTELOCwhitehouse.gov"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTELOCwhitehouse.gov-17"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnmp","href":"./Template:Sfnmp"},"params":{"1a1":{"wt":"LOC"},"2a1":{"wt":"whitehouse.gov"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTELOCwhitehouse.gov_17-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTELOCwhitehouse.gov-17" id="mwkw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwlA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwlQ">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwlg">]</span></span></a></sup></th>
<th colspan="2" id="mwlw" scope="col">Party<sup about="#mwt68" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;group&quot;:&quot;lower-alpha&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-18&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;efn&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Efn&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Reflects the president's political party at the start of their presidency. <th class="unsortable" id="mwoA" scope="col">Election</th>
<th class="unsortable" id="mwoQ" scope="col">Vice President<sup about="#mwt74" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTELOC"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTELOC-20"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfnp","href":"./Template:Sfnp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"LOC"}},"i":0}}]}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTELOC_20-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTELOC-20" id="mwog"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwow"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwpA">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwpQ">]</span></span></a></sup></th></tr>

The elections of 1792 were the first ones in the United States that were contested on anything resembling a partisan basis.{{Sfnmp |''Guide to U.S. Elections''|2010|1pp=197, 272|Nardulli|1992|2p=179}}&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}" id="cite_ref-23" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_note-23" id="mwxQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwxg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwxw">[</span>c<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwyA">]</span></span></a></sup></td></tr>

</section><section aria-labelledby="Notes" data-mw-section-id="3" id="mwB7A"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Notes">Notes</h2></div>
<span about="#mwt712" class="mw-empty-elt" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Notelist","href":"./Template:Notelist"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}' id="mwB7E" typeof="mw:Transclusion"><style about="#mwt713" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Reflist/styles.css"}}' data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1327269900" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles">.mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-2{column-width:30em}.mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-3{column-width:25em}body.skin-vector-2022 .mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-2{column-width:27em}body.skin-vector-2022 .mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-3{column-width:22.5em}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=upper-alpha]{list-style-type:upper-alpha}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=upper-roman]{list-style-type:upper-roman}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=lower-alpha]{list-style-type:lower-alpha}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=lower-greek]{list-style-type:lower-greek}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=lower-roman]{list-style-type:lower-roman}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-upper-alpha .references{list-style-type:upper-alpha}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-upper-roman .references{list-style-type:upper-roman}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-lower-alpha .references{list-style-type:lower-alpha}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-lower-greek .references{list-style-type:lower-greek}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-lower-roman .references{list-style-type:lower-roman}</style></span><div about="#mwt712" id="mwB7I">
<div about="#mwt714" class="mw-references-wrap mw-references-columns" data-mw='{"name":"references","attrs":{"group":"lower-alpha","responsive":"1"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}' id="mwB7M" typeof="mw:Extension/references"><ol class="mw-references references" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwB7Q"><li about="#cite_note-16" data-mw-footnote-number="a" id="cite_note-16"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwB7U"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-16" id="mwB7Y" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB7c">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-16">Presidents are numbered according to uninterrupted periods served by the same person. For example, George Washington served two consecutive terms and is counted as the first president, instead of the first and second, but Grover Cleveland and Donald Trump are counted twice because their two terms were not consecutive. A vice president who temporarily becomes <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Acting_President_of_the_United_States" id="mwB7g" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Acting President of the United States">acting president</a> under the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Twenty-fifth_Amendment_to_the_United_States_Constitution" id="mwB7k" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Twenty-fifth Amendment to the United States Constitution">Twenty-fifth Amendment</a> to the Constitution is not counted, because the president remains in office during such a period.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-18" data-mw-footnote-number="b" id="cite_note-18"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwB7o"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-18" id="mwB7s" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB7w">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-18">Reflects the president's political party at the start of their presidency. Changes during their time in office are noted. Also reflects the vice president's political party unless otherwise noted beside the individual's name.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-23" data-mw-footnote-number="c" id="cite_note-23"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwB70"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-23" id="mwB74" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB78">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-23">Political parties had not been anticipated when the Constitution was drafted, nor did they exist at the time of the first presidential election in 1788–89. When they did develop, during Washington's first term, Adams joined the faction that became the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Federalist_Party" id="mwB8A" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Federalist Party">Federalist Party</a>. The elections of 1792 were the first ones in the United States that were contested on anything resembling a partisan basis.<sup about="#mwt87" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;group&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;name&quot;:&quot;FOOTNOTE''Guide to U.S. Elections''2010197, 272Nardulli1992179&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010197,_272Nardulli1992179-22&quot;}}" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010197,_272Nardulli1992179_22-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010197,_272Nardulli1992179-22" id="mwB8E"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwB8I"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwB8M">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwB8Q">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-26" data-mw-footnote-number="d" id="cite_note-26"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwB8U"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-26" id="mwB8Y" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB8c">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-26">The 1796 presidential election was the first contested American presidential election and the only one in which a president and vice president were elected from opposing political parties. Federalist John Adams was elected president, and Jefferson of the Democratic-Republicans was elected vice president.<sup about="#mwt102" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;group&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;name&quot;:&quot;FOOTNOTE''Guide to U.S. Elections''2010274&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010274-25&quot;}}" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010274_25-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010274-25" id="mwB8g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwB8k"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwB8o">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwB8s">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-diedintraterm-30" data-mw-footnote-number="e" id="cite_note-diedintraterm-30"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwB8w" rel="mw:referencedBy"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-0" id="mwB80"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB84">1</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-1" id="mwB88"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB9A">2</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-2" id="mwB9E"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB9I">3</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-3" id="mwB9M"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB9Q">4</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-4" id="mwB9U"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB9Y">5</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-5" id="mwB9c"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB9g">6</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-6" id="mwB9k"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB9o">7</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-7" id="mwB9s"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB9w">8</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-8" id="mwB90"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB94">9</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-9" id="mwB98"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB-A">10</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-10" id="mwB-E"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB-I">11</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-11" id="mwB-M"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB-Q">12</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-12" id="mwB-U"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB-Y">13</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-13" id="mwB-c"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB-g">14</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-diedintraterm_30-14" id="mwB-k"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB-o">15</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-diedintraterm-30">Died in office<sup about="#mwt126" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTENeale200422"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTENeale200422-29"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTENeale200422_29-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTENeale200422-29" id="mwB-s"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwB-w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwB-0">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwB-4">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-JQAdams-34" data-mw-footnote-number="f" id="cite_note-JQAdams-34"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwB-8"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-JQAdams_34-0" id="mwB_A" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB_E">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-JQAdams-34">Early during John Quincy Adams' term, the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Democratic-Republican_Party" id="mwB_I" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Democratic-Republican Party">Democratic-Republican Party</a> dissolved; his allies in Congress and at the state level were referred to as "Adams' Men" during the Adams presidency. When <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andrew_Jackson" id="mwB_M" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Andrew Jackson">Andrew Jackson</a> became president in 1829, this group became the "Anti-Jackson" <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opposition_(politics)" id="mwB_Q" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Opposition (politics)">opposition</a>, and organized themselves as the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Republican_Party" id="mwB_U" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="National Republican Party">National Republican Party</a>.<sup about="#mwt153" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;group&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;name&quot;:&quot;FOOTNOTE''Guide to U.S. Elections''2010228Goldman1951159&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010228Goldman1951159-33&quot;}}" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010228Goldman1951159_33-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010228Goldman1951159-33" id="mwB_Y"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwB_c"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwB_g">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwB_k">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-36" data-mw-footnote-number="g" id="cite_note-36"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwB_o"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-36" id="mwB_s" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwB_w">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-36">John Calhoun, formerly a Democratic-Republican, founded the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nullifier_Party" id="mwB_0" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Nullifier Party">Nullifier Party</a> in 1828 to oppose the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tariff_of_Abominations" id="mwB_4" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Tariff of Abominations">Tariff of 1828</a> and advance the cause of <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/States'_rights" id="mwB_8" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="States' rights">states' rights</a>, but was brought on as Andrew Jackson's running mate in the 1828 presidential election in an effort to broaden the democratic coalition led by Jackson.<sup about="#mwt159" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;group&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;name&quot;:&quot;FOOTNOTE''Guide to U.S. Elections''2010892Houpt201026, 280&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010892Houpt201026,_280-35&quot;}}" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010892Houpt201026,_280_35-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTE''Guide_to_U.S._Elections''2010892Houpt201026,_280-35" id="mwCAA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCAE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCAI">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCAM">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-resignedintraterm-38" data-mw-footnote-number="h" id="cite_note-resignedintraterm-38"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCAQ" rel="mw:referencedBy"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-resignedintraterm_38-0" id="mwCAU"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCAY">1</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-resignedintraterm_38-1" id="mwCAc"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCAg">2</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-resignedintraterm_38-2" id="mwCAk"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCAo">3</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-resignedintraterm-38">Resigned from office<sup about="#mwt176" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTENeale200422"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTENeale200422-29"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTENeale200422_29-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTENeale200422-29" id="mwCAs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCAw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCA0">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCA4">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-43" data-mw-footnote-number="i" id="cite_note-43"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCA8"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-43" id="mwCBA" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCBE">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-43">John Tyler succeeded to the presidency upon the death of William Henry Harrison.<sup about="#mwt209" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEAbbott201323"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEAbbott201323-42"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEAbbott201323_42-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEAbbott201323-42" id="mwCBI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCBM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCBQ">[</span>34<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCBU">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-JTyler-45" data-mw-footnote-number="j" id="cite_note-JTyler-45"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCBY"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-JTyler_45-0" id="mwCBc" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCBg">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-JTyler-45">John Tyler was elected vice president on the Whig Party ticket in 1840. His policy priorities as president soon proved to be opposed to most of the Whig agenda, and he was expelled from the party five months after assuming office.<sup about="#mwt218" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTECash201834–36"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTECash201834–36-44"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTECash201834–36_44-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTECash201834–36-44" id="mwCBk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCBo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCBs">[</span>35<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCBw">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-50" data-mw-footnote-number="k" id="cite_note-50"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCB0"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-50" id="mwCB4" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCB8">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-50">Millard Fillmore succeeded to the presidency upon the death of Zachary Taylor.<sup about="#mwt254" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEAbbott2005639"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005639-49"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005639_49-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005639-49" id="mwCCA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCCE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCCI">[</span>39<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCCM">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-ALincoln-55" data-mw-footnote-number="l" id="cite_note-ALincoln-55"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCCQ"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-ALincoln_55-0" id="mwCCU" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCCY">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-ALincoln-55">When he ran for reelection in 1864, Republican Abraham Lincoln formed a bipartisan <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Electoral_alliance" id="mwCCc" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Electoral alliance">electoral alliance</a> with <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/War_Democrat" id="mwCCg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="War Democrat">War Democrats</a> by selecting Democrat Andrew Johnson as his running mate, and running on the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Union_Party_(United_States)" id="mwCCk" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="National Union Party (United States)">National Union Party</a> ticket.<sup about="#mwt300" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEMcSeveney1986139"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcSeveney1986139-54"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEMcSeveney1986139_54-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcSeveney1986139-54" id="mwCCo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCCs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCCw">[</span>43<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCC0">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-57" data-mw-footnote-number="m" id="cite_note-57"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCC4"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-57" id="mwCC8" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCDA">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-57">Andrew Johnson succeeded to the presidency upon the death of Abraham Lincoln.<sup about="#mwt312" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTETrefousse2000"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTETrefousse2000-56"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTETrefousse2000_56-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTETrefousse2000-56" id="mwCDE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCDI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCDM">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCDQ">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-AJohnson-58" data-mw-footnote-number="n" id="cite_note-AJohnson-58"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCDU"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-AJohnson_58-0" id="mwCDY" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCDc">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-AJohnson-58">While president, Andrew Johnson tried and failed to build a coalition of loyalists under the National Union banner. Near the end of his presidency, Johnson began reassociating with the Democratic Party.<sup about="#mwt321" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTETrefousse2000"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTETrefousse2000-56"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTETrefousse2000_56-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTETrefousse2000-56" id="mwCDg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCDk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCDo">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCDs">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-64" data-mw-footnote-number="o" id="cite_note-64"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCDw"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-64" id="mwCD0" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCD4">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-64">Chester A. Arthur succeeded to the presidency upon the death of James A. Garfield.<sup about="#mwt369" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEGreenberger2017174–175"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEGreenberger2017174–175-63"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEGreenberger2017174–175_63-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEGreenberger2017174–175-63" id="mwCD8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCEA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCEE">[</span>49<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCEI">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-70" data-mw-footnote-number="p" id="cite_note-70"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCEM"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-70" id="mwCEQ" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCEU">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-70">Theodore Roosevelt succeeded to the presidency upon the death of William McKinley.<sup about="#mwt436" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEAbbott2005639–640"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005639–640-69"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005639–640_69-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005639–640-69" id="mwCEY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCEc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCEg">[</span>54<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCEk">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-76" data-mw-footnote-number="q" id="cite_note-76"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCEo"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-76" id="mwCEs" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCEw">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-76">Calvin Coolidge succeeded to the presidency upon the death of Warren G. Harding.<sup about="#mwt489" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTESenate"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTESenate-75"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTESenate_75-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTESenate-75" id="mwCE0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCE4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCE8">[</span>59<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCFA">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-81" data-mw-footnote-number="r" id="cite_note-81"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCFE"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-81" id="mwCFI" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCFM">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-81">Harry S. Truman succeeded to the presidency upon the death of Franklin D. Roosevelt.<sup about="#mwt529" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEAbbott2005636"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005636-80"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005636_80-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005636-80" id="mwCFQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCFU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCFY">[</span>63<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCFc">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-86" data-mw-footnote-number="s" id="cite_note-86"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCFg"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-86" id="mwCFk" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCFo">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-86">Lyndon B. Johnson succeeded to the presidency upon the death of John F. Kennedy.<sup about="#mwt569" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEAbbott2005633"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005633-85"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005633_85-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEAbbott2005633-85" id="mwCFs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCFw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCF0">[</span>67<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCF4">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-VP25th-88" data-mw-footnote-number="t" id="cite_note-VP25th-88"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCF8" rel="mw:referencedBy"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-VP25th_88-0" id="mwCGA"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCGE">1</span></a> <a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-VP25th_88-1" id="mwCGI"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCGM">2</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-VP25th-88">Appointed as vice president under terms of the <a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Twenty-fifth_Amendment_to_the_United_States_Constitution" id="mwCGQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Twenty-fifth Amendment to the United States Constitution">Twenty-fifth Amendment</a>, Section 2<sup about="#mwt596" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTENeale200422"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTENeale200422-29"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTENeale200422_29-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTENeale200422-29" id="mwCGU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCGY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCGc">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCGg">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-90" data-mw-footnote-number="u" id="cite_note-90"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwCGk"><a data-mw-group="lower-alpha" href="#cite_ref-90" id="mwCGo" rel="mw:referencedBy"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwCGs">↑</span></a></span> <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-90">Gerald Ford succeeded to the presidency upon the resignation of Richard Nixon. Even though Ford served out the remainder of Nixon's second term, he was never elected to the presidency or vice presidency.<sup about="#mwt608" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEGreene2013"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEGreene2013-89"}}' id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEGreene2013_89-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEGreene2013-89" id="mwCGw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCG0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCG4">[</span>69<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCG8">]</span></span></a></sup></span></li>

<p><b>Expert studies</b></p>
<ul><li><link about="#mwt727" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Citation/CS1/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}' href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1333433106" rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles"/><cite class="citation journal cs1" id="CITEREFAbbott2005"><a href="//en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Philip_Abbott_(academic)" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Philip Abbott (academic)">Abbott, Philip</a> (2005).
